## Review rule

Read the verse with at least the preceding four verses and following verse. Classify each masculine pronoun as: (A) unmistakably YHWH/Elohim/God/divine title, propose recast; (B) Yeshua/Messiah, retain; (C) actual male human/angelic speaker, retain; (D) generic human, separately review under inclusive-human policy; (E) ambiguous/poetic/quoted, retain unless independently resolved.

Full candidate inventory: `change-logs/reports/2026-11-25-divine-pronoun-residual-candidates.tsv` and machine JSON: `change-logs/reports/2026-11-25-divine-pronoun-residual-audit.json`.
